If you violate any of them the page will fail to load\nor display a blank screen with console errors. The majority of past failures\nhave been caused by ignoring one of these rules. Re-read this block before you\nstart writing code and again before you finish.\n\nH1. **FILES THAT MUST EXIST.** You must create exactly these three files and\n    every one of them must be non-empty and syntactically valid JavaScript/HTML:\n        - `index.html`\n        - `elevator.js`\n        - `person.js`\n    `index.html` references `elevator.js` and `person.js` via `<script src=...>`.\n    If either .js file is missing, the browser will log `404 Not Found` and the\n    page will be blank. Before finishing, use your file-read tool to open each\n    of the three files and visually confirm they contain complete code.\n\nH2. **NO ES6 MODULES. NO `import`. NO `export`.** This is a plain-script\n    project using global variables. Writing `import { createPerson } from ...`\n    or `export function ...` will produce the console error\n    `SyntaxError: Cannot use import statement outside a module` and the whole\n    simulation will fail to start. Communicate between files using global\n    functions/variables attached to `window` or declared at the top level. Do\n    not add `type=\"module\"` to any `<script>` tag.\n\nH3. **THE SIMULATION MUST AUTO-START ON PAGE LOAD.** At the bottom of\n    `elevator.js`, after all functions are declared, invoke your `init()` /\n    `main()` / `startSimulation()` function at the top level (either directly,\n    or inside a `window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ...)` handler).\n    Do NOT only attach it to `window.foo` and wait for something to call it.\n    If nothing runs at load time the page will be blank with zero errors,\n    which is the worst kind of failure to debug.\n\nH4. **ZERO CONSOLE ERRORS AT STARTUP.** Open the browser's DevTools console\n    mentally as you write: any `ReferenceError`, `TypeError`, `SyntaxError`,\n    or 404 on a script file at startup counts as a total failure. Code\n    defensively: before reading `someObject.someProp.someField`, make sure\n    `someObject.someProp` was actually assigned.\n\nH5. **NAMING CONTRACT — use these exact variable names, spelled exactly this way:**\n        - `scene`, `camera`, `renderer`, `controls` — top-level Three.js objects\n        - `elevatorCar` — the THREE.Group representing the elevator (NOT\n          `elevatorGroup`, NOT `lift`, NOT `car`). Doors and passengers are\n          added as children of `elevatorCar`.\n        - `elevatorCar.leftDoor` and `elevatorCar.rightDoor` — direct THREE.Mesh\n          references stored as properties on `elevatorCar` for animation access.\n        - `people` — array of person objects\n    A common past failure: the model created a local `elevatorGroup` inside\n    `createElevator()` but never assigned it to the global `elevatorCar`, then\n    later did `elevatorCar.leftDoor = ...` and crashed with\n    `Cannot set properties of undefined`. Either declare `elevatorCar` as a\n    global `let` and assign to it inside the factory, OR have the factory\n    `return` the group and do `elevatorCar = createElevator();` at the call site.\n\nH6. **CONSTANTS ARE TOP-LEVEL `const` DECLARATIONS — NOT wrapped in a config object.**\n    Write exactly this shape at the top of `elevator.js`:\n        ```\n        const FLOOR_HEIGHT = 3;\n        const FLOOR_COUNT = 6;\n        const BUILDING_WIDTH = 20;\n        const BUILDING_DEPTH = 15;\n        const SHAFT_WIDTH = 5;\n        const SHAFT_DEPTH = 5;\n        const ELEVATOR_SPEED = 2;\n        const PERSON_MOVE_SPEED = 1;\n        ```\n    Do NOT wrap them in `const config = { FLOOR_COUNT: 6, ... }`. A past failure\n    mixed styles — declared `config.FLOOR_COUNT` but then referenced bare\n    `FLOOR_COUNT` later, causing `ReferenceError: FLOOR_COUNT is not defined`.\n\nH7. **`person.userData` CONTRACT.** Every person object returned by\n    `createPerson()` in `person.js` must have this structure populated before\n    it is returned:\n        ```\n        person.userData = {\n            leftLeg:  <THREE.Object3D reference to the left leg mesh>,\n            rightLeg: <THREE.Object3D reference to the right leg mesh>,\n            isWalking: false\n        };\n        ```\n    The animation loop in `elevator.js` reads these fields every frame. If\n    `userData.leftLeg` is missing you will see hundreds of repeated\n    `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined` errors.\n\nH8. **REPARENTING PRESERVES WORLD TRANSFORM — USE `.attach()`, NOT `.add()`.**\n    This is the single most common visual bug and has affected nearly every\n    prior model. When you move the person between `scene` and `elevatorCar`,\n    you MUST use `THREE.Object3D.attach()`:\n        ```\n        // BOARDING (scene → elevator): preserves world position\n        elevatorCar.attach(person);\n\n        // EXITING (elevator → scene): preserves world position\n        scene.attach(person);\n        ```\n    Do NOT use `elevatorCar.add(person)` or `scene.add(person)` for reparenting\n    a person that already has a meaningful position in the world. `.add()`\n    keeps the raw `.position` numbers unchanged, which means the coordinate\n    frame silently switches underneath the object.\n\n    Concrete failure this rule prevents: after the elevator travels up, if you\n    do `scene.add(person)` while the person is a child of the elevator at\n    local `y = 0`, the person's `position.y` is still `0` but is now interpreted\n    in world coordinates — the passenger teleports down to the ground floor\n    (or walks out into mid-air above the roof in the reverse case).\n\n    If for some reason you cannot use `.attach()`, you must manually convert\n    before reparenting:\n        ```\n        const worldPos = new THREE.Vector3();\n        person.getWorldPosition(worldPos);\n        elevatorCar.remove(person);\n        scene.add(person);\n        person.position.copy(worldPos);    // restore world-space position\n        ```\n    Do NOT try to \"fix it later\" by setting `person.position.y = floorY`\n    after the fact — that causes a visible teleport/snap.\n\n    Additional invariant: at any moment, the person's WORLD y-coordinate must\n    equal the Y of the floor they are currently standing on (or traveling\n    through inside the elevator). Sanity-check this: right before the exit\n    animation starts, `person.getWorldPosition(v).y` should equal the\n    destination floor's Y. After `scene.attach(person)`, it should still equal\n    that value.\n\n═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n## Core Requirements\n═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n### Visual Structure\n1. **Building**:\n   - Doors meet in the middle when closing\n     - Doors should be slightly more opaque (0.7) than the frame\n   - Solid back wall, transparent side walls\n   - Position at floor level, not floating\n\n3. **People**:\n   - Simple 3D humanoid figures made from Three.js primitives\n   - CRITICAL: Feet must align exactly with floor level (not protruding through floor)\n   - CRITICAL: Arms must hang DOWN from shoulders, not up from hips\n   - Body structure from bottom to top: legs → torso → head, with arms at shoulder level\n   - Must populate `person.userData` per rule H7 above.\n\n### Positioning & Movement\n\n4. **Person Positioning**:\n   - People wait IN FRONT of the elevator doors (on positive Z-axis)\n   - People must FACE the elevator (rotate 180° to look toward doors)\n   - When boarding/exiting, people walk FORWARD through the doors (not sideways)\n   - Never position people to the side of the elevator\n\n5. **Walking Animation**:\n   - Animate legs with alternating swing motion during walking\n   - Use sine wave for smooth leg rotation on X-axis\n   - Reset legs to standing position when stationary\n   - The legs should pivot from the hips/body, not the knees/mid-leg\n   - The animation loop reads `person.userData.leftLeg` / `rightLeg` / `isWalking`\n     — set `isWalking = true` when the person starts walking, `false` when they stop.\n\n6. **Door Animation**:\n   - Doors open BEFORE person enters/exits\n   - Doors close AFTER person is fully inside/outside\n   - Add brief delays (300ms) between steps for realism\n\nScene Graph & Parent-Child Relationships:\n  - When person boards the elevator, they must become a child of `elevatorCar`\n    (not the scene) so they travel with it during vertical movement.\n  - When the person exits, they must be re-parented back to `scene`.\n  - **Use `elevatorCar.attach(person)` on boarding and `scene.attach(person)`\n    on exit — NOT `.add()`.** See rule H8. Elevator moves to pickup floor\n2. Doors open (sliding animation)\n3. Person walks forward into elevator (with leg animation) then becomes a child of elevator object\n4. Doors close\n5. Elevator travels to destination\n6. Doors open at destination\n7. Person walks forward to waiting spot (with leg animation) then is removed from elevator and added back to scene\n8. Doors close\n\n### Transparency Rendering (CRITICAL)\n\n7. **Three.js Transparency Setup**:\n   - Enable `renderer.sortObjects = true` for proper depth sorting\n   - Add `renderer.alpha = true` to WebGL renderer\n   - ALL transparent materials MUST have `depthWrite: false` to prevent z-fighting\n   - ALL transparent materials should have `side: THREE.DoubleSide`\n   - Use `renderOrder` property: building=0, elevator=1\n   - Update empty floor after each move\n\n9. **Camera & Controls**:\n   - Position camera at (25, 25, 25) looking at building center\n   - Use OrbitControls for user interaction\n   - Ensure all objects remain visible during rotation\n\n### Technical Specifications\n\n10. **Files** (see H1 above for the hard requirement):\n   - slightly smaller than shaft\n    var elevWidth = SHAFT_WIDTH - 0.4;\n    var elevDepth = SHAFT_DEPTH - 0.4;\n    var frameThickness = 0.15;\n    var totalHeight = FLOOR_COUNT * FLOOR_HEIGHT;\n\n    // Bottom plate (solid)\n    var bottomGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(elevWidth, frameThickness, elevDepth);\n    var bottomPlate = new THREE.Mesh(bottomGeo, frameMat);\n    bottomPlate.position.y = 0;\n    elevatorCar.add(bottomPlate);\n\n    // Top plate\n    var topPlate = new THREE.Mesh(bottomGeo.clone(), frameMat);\n    topPlate.position.y = totalHeight;\n    elevatorCar.add(topPlate);\n\n    // Left wall (transparent)\n    var sideWallMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xffff00, transparent: true, opacity: 0.15, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide });\n    var sideWallGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(frameThickness, totalHeight, elevDepth);\n\n    var leftWall = new THREE.Mesh(sideWallGeo, sideWallMat);\n    leftWall.position.set(-elevWidth / 2 + frameThickness / 2, totalHeight / 2, 0);\n    elevatorCar.add(leftWall);\n\n    // Right wall (transparent)\n    var rightWall = new THREE.Mesh(sideWallGeo.clone(), sideWallMat);\n    rightWall.position.set(elevWidth / 2 - frameThickness / 2, totalHeight / 2, 0);\n    elevatorCar.add(rightWall);\n\n    // Back wall (solid)\n    var backWallGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(elevWidth, totalHeight, frameThickness);\n    var backWall = new THREE.Mesh(backWallGeo, frameMat);\n    backWall.position.set(0, totalHeight / 2, -elevDepth / 2 + frameThickness / 2);\n    elevatorCar.add(backWall);\n\n    // === DOORS (on the front side of the elevator) ===\n    var doorWidth = elevWidth / 2 - frameThickness / 4, totalHeight / 2, elevDepth / 2 - frameThickness / 2);\n    rightDoor.position.set(doorWidth / 2 + frameThickness / 4, totalHeight / 2, elevDepth / 2 - frameThickness / 2);\n\n    // Elevator cables (visual)\n    var cableMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x888888 });\n    for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) {\n        var cableGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.05, totalHeight + 10, 0.05);\n        var cable = new THREE.Mesh(cableGeo, cableMat);\n        var cx = -elevWidth / 2 + (i % 2 === 0 ? 0 : elevWidth - 0.35; // How far doors slide outward\n\n    function step() {\n        if (doorState !== 'opening') return false;\n        var elapsed = Date.now() - start;\n        var t = Math.min(elapsed / animDuration, 1);\n\n        if (t >= 1) {\n            doorState = 'opened';\n            doorsOpenProgress = 1;\n            // Position doors fully open\n            elevatorCar.leftDoor.position.x = -openXOffset;\n            elevatorCar.rightDoor.position.x = openXOffset;\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Interpolate: doors move outward from center\n        var leftX = (-doorWidth / 2) + t * openXOffset;\n        var rightX = (doorWidth / 2) - 2 * t) * t;\n}\n\nfunction animateLinear(current, target, durationMs) {\n    var start = Date.now();\n    var elapsed = 0;\n    var animating = true;\n\n    function step() {\n        if (!animating) return false;\n        elapsed = Date.now() - start;\n        var t = Math.min(elapsed / (durationMs * speedMultiplier), 1);\n        current = target * t;\n        if (t >= 1) {\n            animating = false;\n            current = target;\n        }\n        return animating;\n    }\n\n    function getProgress() {\n        var t = Math.min(elapsed / (durationMs * speedMultiplier), 1);\n        return current / target;\n    }\n\n    return { step: step, progress: function() { return getProgress(); }, isAnimating: function() { return animating; } };\n}\n\n// === BUILDING CREATION ===\nfunction createBuilding() {\n    var floorMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xcccccc, transparent: true, opacity: 0.3, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide });\n    var wallMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x9999ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.15, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide });\n\n    // Ground floor - solid\n    var groundGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(BUILDING_WIDTH, 0.3, BUILDING_DEPTH);\n    var groundMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xcccccc });\n    var ground = new THREE.Mesh(groundGeo, groundMat);\n    ground.position.set(0, -0.15, 0);\n    scene.add(ground);\n\n    // Roof (solid)\n    var roofGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(BUILDING_WIDTH, 0.3, BUILDING_DEPTH);\n    var roofMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xcccccc });\n    var roof = new THREE.Mesh(roofGeo, roofMat);\n    roof.position.set(0, TOTAL_HEIGHT + 0.15, 0);\n    scene.add(roof);\n\n    // Transparent floors (floors 1-5) with shaft cutout - use 4 separate pieces around the shaft\n    for (var i = 1; i < FLOOR_COUNT; i++) {\n        var floorY = i * FLOOR_HEIGHT;\n        var halfBuildingX = BUILDING_WIDTH / 2;\n        var halfShaftX = SHAFT_WIDTH / 2;\n        var halfBuildingZ = BUILDING_DEPTH / 2;\n        var halfShaftZ = SHAFT_DEPTH / 2;\n\n        // Left section of floor (left side of shaft)\n        var leftFloorGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(halfBuildingX - halfShaftX, 0.15, BUILDING_DEPTH);\n        var rightFloor = new THREE.Mesh(rightGeo, floorMat);\n        var rightCenterX = halfShaftX + (halfBuildingX - halfShaftX) / 2;\n        rightFloor.position.set(rightCenterX, floorY, 0);\n        scene.add(rightFloor);\n\n        // Front section of floor (in front of shaft along Z)\n        var frontGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(SHAFT_WIDTH, 0.15, halfBuildingZ - halfShaftZ);\n        var frontFloor = new THREE.Mesh(frontGeo, floorMat);\n        var frontCenterZ = halfShaftZ + (halfBuildingZ - halfShaftZ) / 2;\n        frontFloor.position.set(0, floorY, frontCenterZ);\n        scene.add(frontFloor);\n\n        // Back section of floor (behind shaft along Z)\n        var backGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(SHAFT_WIDTH, 0.15, halfBuildingZ - split into pieces around the shaft opening\n    // Left part of front wall\n    var leftWallGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(halfBuildingX - halfShaftX, totalHeight, 0.2);\n    var leftWall = new THREE.Mesh(leftWallGeo, wallMat);\n    leftWall.position.set(-(halfShaftX + (halfBuildingX - positive Z) ===\n    var doorHeight = TOTAL_HEIGHT;\n\n    // Left door - closed state: positioned to meet at center from left side\n    var leftDoorGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(DOOR_WIDTH, doorHeight, FRAME_THICKNESS);\n    var leftDoor = new THREE.Mesh(leftDoorGeo, doorMat);\n    elevatorCar.add(leftDoor);\n    elevatorCar.leftDoor = leftDoor; // H5: store reference\n\n    // Right door - closed state: positioned to meet at center from right side\n    var rightDoorGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(DOOR_WIDTH, doorHeight, FRAME_THICKNESS);\n    var rightDoor = new THREE.Mesh(rightDoorGeo, doorMat);\n    elevatorCar.add(rightDoor);\n    elevatorCar.rightDoor = rightDoor; // H5: store reference\n\n    // Position doors in the closed state (meeting at center)\n    leftDoor.position.set(-DOOR_WIDTH / 2 - FRAME_THICKNESS / 4, TOTAL_HEIGHT / 2, ELEV_DEPTH / 2);\n    rightDoor.position.set(DOOR_WIDTH / 2 + FRAME_THICKNESS / 4, TOTAL_HEIGHT / 2, ELEV_DEPTH / 2);\n\n    // Elevator bottom plate (solid)\n    var elevBottomGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(ELEV_WIDTH - FRAME_THICKNESS * 2, 0.15, ELEV_DEPTH - FRAME_THICKNESS * 2);\n    var elevBottom = new THREE.Mesh(elevBottomGeo, frameMat);\n    elevBottom.position.y = 0;\n    elevatorCar.add(elevBottom);\n\n    // Elevator top plate (solid)\n    var elevTop = new THREE.Mesh(elevBottom.clone(), frameMat);\n    elevTop.position.y = TOTAL_HEIGHT - FRAME_THICKNESS * 2;\n    elevatorCar.add(elevTop);\n\n    // Left wall (transparent)\n    var sideWallGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(FRAME_THICKNESS, TOTAL_HEIGHT - FRAME_THICKNESS * 2, ELEV_DEPTH - FRAME_THICKNESS * 2);\n    var leftSide = new THREE.Mesh(sideWallGeo, sideWallMat);\n    leftSide.position.set(-ELEV_WIDTH / 2 + FRAME_THICKNESS, (TOTAL_HEIGHT - FRAME_THICKNESS, (TOTAL_HEIGHT - FRAME_THICKNESS * 2) / 2, 0);\n    elevatorCar.add(rightSide);\n\n    // Back wall (solid)\n    var backWallGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(ELEV_WIDTH - FRAME_THICKNESS * 3, TOTAL_HEIGHT - FRAME_THICKNESS * 2, FRAME_THICKNESS);\n    var backWall = new THREE.Mesh(backWallGeo, frameMat);\n    backWall.position.set(0, (TOTAL_HEIGHT - FRAME_THICKNESS * 2) / 2, -ELEV_DEPTH / 2 + FRAME_THICKNESS);\n    elevatorCar.add(backWall);\n\n    // Elevator cables (visual)\n    var cableMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x888888 });\n    for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) {\n        var cableGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.05, TOTAL_HEIGHT + 10, 0.05);\n        var cable = new THREE.Mesh(cableGeo, cableMat);\n        var cx = -ELEV_WIDTH / 2 + (i % 2 === 0 ? FRAME_THICKNESS : ELEV_WIDTH - FRAME_THICKNESS);\n        var cz = -(ELEV_DEPTH / 4) + Math.floor(i / 2) * (ELEV_DEPTH / 2);\n        cable.position.set(cx, TOTAL_HEIGHT + (TOTAL_HEIGHT + 10) / 2, cz);\n        elevatorCar.add(cable);\n    }\n\n    // Set renderOrder for transparency (H7)\n    elevatorCar.renderOrder = 1;\n}\n\n// === DOOR ANIMATION ===\nfunction animateDoorsOpen(callback) {\n    doorState = 'opening';\n    doorsOpenProgress = 0;\n\n    var animDuration = 600 / speedMultiplier; // Time in ms for full open\n    var start = Date.now();\n\n    function animateLoop() {\n        if (doorState !== 'opening') return;\n        var elapsed = Date.now() - start;\n        var t = Math.min(elapsed / animDuration, 1);\n        var easedT = easeInOut(t);\n\n        // Doors move outward from center\n        leftDoorX = -DOOR_WIDTH / 2 + easedT * DOOR_OPEN_DISTANCE;\n        rightDoorX = DOOR_WIDTH / 2 - easedT * DOOR_OPEN_DISTANCE;\n\n        if (t >= 1) {\n            doorState = 'opened';\n            doorsOpenProgress = 1;\n            leftDoorX = DOOR_OPEN_DISTANCE;\n            rightDoorX = DOOR_OPEN_DISTANCE;\n            // Callback after brief delay for realism (H7 spec: 300ms between steps)\n            setTimeout(callback, 200 / speedMultiplier);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        requestAnimationFrame(animateLoop);\n    }\n    animateLoop();\n}\n\nfunction animateDoorsClose(callback) {\n    doorState = 'closing';\n\n    var animDuration = 600 / speedMultiplier; // Time in ms for full close\n    var start = Date.now();\n\n    function animateLoop() {\n        if (doorState !== 'closing') return;\n        var elapsed = Date.now() - start;\n        var t = Math.min(elapsed / animDuration, 1);\n        var easedT = easeInOut(t);\n\n        // Doors move inward toward center\n        leftDoorX = DOOR_OPEN_DISTANCE * (1 - easedT) - DOOR_WIDTH / 2 + FRAME_THICKNESS / 4;\n        rightDoorX = DOOR_OPEN_DISTANCE * (1 - easedT) + DOOR_WIDTH / 2 - FRAME_THICKNESS / 4;\n\n        if (t >= 1) {\n            doorState = 'closed';\n            doorsOpenProgress = 0;\n            // Position doors closed (meeting at center)\n            leftDoorX = -DOOR_WIDTH / 2 + FRAME_THICKNESS / 4;\n            rightDoorX = DOOR_WIDTH / 2 - FRAME_THICKNESS / 4;\n            // Callback after brief delay for realism (H7 spec: 300ms between steps)\n            setTimeout(callback, 200 / speedMultiplier);\n            return;\n        }\n\n        requestAnimationFrame(animateLoop);\n    }\n    animateLoop();\n}\n\n// Global variables to track door positions during animation\nvar leftDoorX = -DOOR_WIDTH / 2 + FRAME_THICKNESS / 4;\nvar rightDoorX = DOOR_WIDTH / 2 - FRAME_THICKNESS / 4;\n\n// === PERSON POSITIONING HELPERS ===\nfunction getPersonFloorY(person) {\n    if (elevatorCar.contains(person)) {\n        // Person is inside the elevator car at local y=0, which corresponds to world floor level\n        return elevatorCurrentFloor * FLOOR_HEIGHT;\n    } else {\n        // Find which floor based on person's world Y position\n        var pPos = new THREE.Vector3();\n        person.getWorldPosition(pPos);\n        for (var i = 0; i < FLOOR_COUNT; i++) {\n            if (i === emptyFloorIndex) continue;\n            if (Math.abs(pPos.y - i * FLOOR_HEIGHT) < 1.5) {\n                return i * FLOOR_HEIGHT;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return 0; // Default to ground floor\n}\n\n// Where people wait: in front of elevator doors (positive Z), facing the elevator\nfunction getWaitingZPosition() {\n    return ELEV_DEPTH / 2 + 1.5; // In front of the door opening\n}\n\n// === BOARDING SEQUENCE (H8: use .attach() for reparenting) ===\nfunction sequenceBoard(personIndex, callback) {\n    var person = people[personIndex];\n    if (!person || !isAnimating) return;\n\n    function step1_DoorsOpen() {\n        animateDoorsOpen(step2_WalkForward);\n    }\n\n    function step2_WalkForward() {\n        // Make person walk toward elevator (forward through doors, decreasing Z from waiting position)\n        if (!person.userData.leftLeg || !person.userData.rightLeg) return;\n        person.userData.isWalking = true;\n        person.userData.walkTime = 0;\n\n        var startZ = getWaitingZPosition();\n        var targetZ = ELEV_DEPTH / 2 - 0.3; // Just inside the door opening\n\n        function animateWalk() {\n            if (!person.userData.isWalking && person.userData.walkTime >= walkDuration) return;\n\n            var t = Math.min(person.userData.walkTime / walkDuration, 1);\n            person.position.z = startZ + (targetZ - startZ) * t;\n\n            if (person.userData.walkTime >= walkDuration && person.userData.isWalking) {\n                person.userData.isWalking = false;\n                person.position.z = targetZ;\n                // Now board the elevator: reparent using .attach() (H8)\n                elevatorCar.attach(person);\n                animateDoorsClose(step4_BeStill);\n            } else {\n                requestAnimationFrame(animateWalk);\n            }\n        }\n\n        var walkDuration = Math.abs(targetZ - startZ) / PERSON_MOVE_SPEED;\n        person.userData.walkTime += 16 / speedMultiplier; // ~60fps increment, adjusted by speed\n        animateWalk();\n    }\n\n    function step4_BeStill() {\n        callback(null, { type: 'boarded' });\n    }\n\n    step1_DoorsOpen();\n}\n\n// === EXITING SEQUENCE (H8: use scene.attach() for reparenting) ===\nfunction sequenceExit(personIndex, callback) {\n    var person = people[personIndex];\n    if (!person || !isAnimating) return;\n\n    function step1_DoorsOpen() {\n        animateDoorsOpen(step2_WalkForward);\n    }\n\n    function step2_WalkForward() {\n        // Make person walk forward out of elevator (increasing Z from inside to outside)\n        if (!person.userData.leftLeg || !person.userData.rightLeg) return;\n        person.userData.isWalking = true;\n        person.userData.walkTime = 0;\n\n        var startZ = ELEV_DEPTH / 2 - 0.3; // Inside the door\n        var targetZ = getWaitingZPosition(); // Outside in front of elevator\n\n        function animateWalk() {\n            if (!person.userData.isWalking && person.userData.walkTime >= walkDuration) return;\n\n            var t = Math.min(person.userData.walkTime / walkDuration, 1);\n            person.position.z = startZ + (targetZ - startZ) * t;\n\n            if (person.userData.walkTime >= walkDuration && person.userData.isWalking) {\n                person.userData.isWalking = false;\n                person.position.z = targetZ;\n                // Now exit the elevator: reparent using .attach() (H8)\n                scene.attach(person);\n                animateDoorsClose(step4_BeStill);\n            } else {\n                requestAnimationFrame(animateWalk);\n            }\n        }\n\n        var walkDuration = Math.abs(targetZ - startZ) / PERSON_MOVE_SPEED;\n        person.userData.walkTime += 16 / speedMultiplier; // ~60fps increment, adjusted by speed\n        animateWalk();\n    }\n\n    function step4_BeStill() {\n        callback(null, { type: 'exited' });\n    }\n\n    step1_DoorsOpen();\n}\n\n// === ELEVATOR MOVEMENT ===\nfunction moveElevatorToFloor(targetFloor, callback) {\n    if (targetFloor < 0 || targetFloor >= FLOOR_COUNT) return;\n\n    elevatorTargetFloor = targetFloor;\n    var startY = elevatorCurrentFloor * FLOOR_HEIGHT;\n    var endY = targetFloor * FLOOR_HEIGHT;\n    var durationMs = Math.abs(endY - startY) / ELEVATOR_SPEED; // seconds converted to ms\n\n    function animateMove() {\n        var elapsed = Date.now();\n        if (elapsed - startMoveTime >= durationMs * speedMultiplier) {\n            elevatorCar.position.y = endY;\n            elevatorCurrentFloor = targetFloor;\n            callback(null, { type: 'arrived' });\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Linear interpolation between startY and endY\n        var t = Math.min((elapsed - startMoveTime) / (durationMs * speedMultiplier), 1);\n        elevatorCar.position.y = startY + (endY - startY) * t;\n        requestAnimationFrame(animateMove);\n    }\n\n    var startMoveTime = Date.now();\n    animateMove();\n}\n\n// === SIMULATION LOGIC ===\nfunction pickNextDestination() {\n    // Pick a random person who is NOT in the empty floor to move\n    var availablePeople = [];\n    for (var i = 0; i < people.length; i++) {\n        if (!elevatorCar.contains(people[i]) && i !== emptyFloorIndex) {\n            availablePeople.push(i);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (availablePeople.length === 0) return null;\n\n    var personIndex = availablePeople[Math.floor(Math.random() * availablePeople.length)];\n    var destinationFloor = emptyFloorIndex;\n\n    // Determine which floor the person is currently on\n    var startFloor = getPersonCurrentFloor(personIndex);\n\n    return {\n        person: personIndex,\n        startFloor: startFloor,\n        destination: destinationFloor\n    };\n}\n\nfunction getPersonCurrentFloor(personIndex) {\n    var person = people[personIndex];\n    if (elevatorCar.contains(person)) {\n        return person.getWorldPosition(pPos);\n            if (Math.abs(pPos.y - i * FLOOR_HEIGHT) < 1.5) {\n                return i;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return elevatorCurrentFloor;\n}\n\n// === MAIN SIMULATION STATE MACHINE ===\nvar currentPerson = null;\nvar personDestinationFloor = null;\n\nfunction startSimulationSequence() {\n    if (isAnimating) return; // Already animating\n\n    var next = pickNextDestination();\n    if (!next) return;\n\n    currentPerson = next.person;\n    personDestinationFloor = next.destination;\n\n    // Elevator needs to be at the pickup floor before boarding can start\n    if (elevatorTargetFloor === null || elevatorCar.position.y !== next.startFloor * FLOOR_HEIGHT + 0.15) {\n        // Move elevator to pickup floor first, then board\n        currentSimulationStep = 'moving';\n        moveElevatorToFloor(next.startFloor, function(err, data) {\n            if (data.type === 'arrived') {\n                startBoardingSequence();\n            }\n        });\n    } else {\n        // Elevator is already at the pickup floor\n        startBoardingSequence();\n    }\n}\n\nfunction startBoardingSequence() {\n    currentSimulationStep = 'boarding';\n    sequenceBoard(currentPerson, function(err, data) {\n        if (data.type === 'boarded') {\n            // Now move elevator to destination floor\n            isAnimating = true;\n            currentSimulationStep = 'moving';\n            moveElevatorToFloor(personDestinationFloor, function(err, data) {\n                startExitingSequence();\n            });\n        } else if           isAnimating = false;\n            currentSimulationStep = 'idle';\n\n            // Move to new random destination after a pause\n            setTimeout(function() {\n                startSimulationSequence();\n            }, 800 / speedMultiplier);\n        } else if (err) {\n            console.error('Exiting failed:', err);\n        }\n    });\n}\n\n// === CAMERA SETUP ===\nfunction createCamera() {\n    camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000);\n    camera.position.set(30, 30, 30);\n\n    // Look at building center for better framing (H9)\n    var centerX = 0;\n    var centerY = TOTAL_HEIGHT / 2;\n    var centerZ = 0;\n    camera.lookAt(new THREE.Vector3(centerX, centerY, centerZ));\n\n    controls = new (p.userData.isWalking) {\n            // Walking: alternate leg swing using sine wave on X-axis (H7)\n            p.userData.walkTime += delta * 5;\n            p.userData.leftLeg.rotation.x =
